However, my last real Linux experience was with Ubuntu about 20 years ago.\n- Internet connection: 250\u002F40 Mbps DSL with a public (dynamic) IPv4 address and a FRITZ!Box 7590 router, which supports WireGuard VPN.\n\n**Here are my main questions:**\n1. Which operating system would you recommend? I've been looking into TrueNAS Community Edition and OpenMediaVault, but I'm open to other suggestions. Ease of use, stability, and low maintenance are important to me.\n2. Would a prebuilt NAS be a good choice? I've been considering devices like the UGREEN DXP4800 Plus and TerraMaster F4-424 Pro. Can their proprietary operating systems be replaced with something open-source without losing important functionality, such as fan control, power management, or hardware monitoring? Are there particular models known to work especially well with TrueNAS or OpenMediaVault?\n3. How much hardware performance do I actually need? Given my relatively modest requirements, would something like an Intel N100 already be sufficient, or would it make sense to invest in a more powerful CPU and additional RAM for future flexibility?\n4. To do this, paste the following into the search field of your instance: <code class="user-select-all"><a href="/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection" class="__cf_email__" data-cfemail="1435677178727c7b6760717054787179796d3a637b667870">[email&#160;protected]</a></code></div></div></div></section><section class="card border-secondary mb-3" id="sidebarInfo"><div class="card-body"><div class="md-div"><p dir="auto">A place to share alternatives to popular online services that can be self-hosted without giving up privacy or locking you into a service you don’t control.</p>
<p dir="auto">Rules:</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://lemmy.world/post/48725669" rel="noopener nofollow">Detailed Rules Post</a></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Be civil.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">No spam.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Posts are to be related to self-hosting.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Don’t duplicate the full text of your blog or readme if you’re providing a link.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Submission headline should match the article title.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">No trolling.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Promotion posts require active participation, with an account that is at least 30 days old. F/LOSS without a paywall has exceptions, with requirements.   See the <a href="https://lemmy.world/post/48725669" rel="noopener nofollow">rules link</a> for details.  Tags [CBH] or [AIP] are <strong>required</strong>, see the links in Rule 8 for details.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">AI-related discussions and AI-involved promotional posts have additional requirements for tagging, as noted in Rule 7 and the <a href="https://lemmy.world/post/49151085" rel="noopener nofollow">AI &amp; Promotional Post Expanded Rules</a> post, and find <a href="https://lemmy.world/post/49320548" rel="noopener nofollow">example disclosures here</a>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
